- **read-article**: 读取小红书笔记或微信公众号文章链接的正文，输出 Markdown。用户提供 mp.weixin.qq.com、xiaohongshu.com、xhslink.com 链接并要求阅读或总结该文章时使用；抓取须走本 skill 的本机直抓脚本，云端出口会被风控拦截。 ([sageri/agent-skills](https://github.com/sageri/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/read-article))

Use whenever asked to read, summarize, check, or consult a paper, preprint, or arXiv posting. - **read-arxiv-paper**: 读取 arxiv 论文的 LaTeX 源码并生成结构化中文摘要或中文博客 JSON。当用户给出 arxiv URL（如 https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07372）并要求阅读、总结、提炼论文观点时，使用默认工作流生成 ./knowledge/summary_{tag}.md；当用户要求生成中文博客、输出 JSON、保留图表并输出到测试工作区时，启用 read-paper 工作流。 ([LSTM-Kirigaya/jinhui-skills](https://github.com/LSTM-Kirigaya/jinhui-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/read-arxiv-paper) · ★25)
- **read-arxiv-paper**: Use this skill when asked to read, analyze, or summarize an arxiv paper given an arxiv URL or paper ID. - **read-arxiv-paper**: Ingest an arXiv paper into the project reference library. Read the full text from alphaXiv markdown (the NOTES.md base), copy exact equations from ar5iv / arXiv-HTML MathML alttext, and pull the arXiv LaTeX e-print source for TikZ diagram code, into references/papers/&lt;slug&gt;/, then write a structured NOTES.md. Use whenever the user gives an arXiv/alphaXiv ID or URL, or asks to "ingest", "read", "summarize", or "add" a paper. - **read-arxiv-paper**: Use this skill when asked to read an arxiv paper given an arxiv URL or alphaxiv URL. Can also be invoked with /read-arxiv-paper. ([cywinski/dotfiles](https://github.com/cywinski/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/read-arxiv-paper) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **read-arxiv-paper**: Fetch and deeply read a SINGLE arxiv paper from its exact LaTeX source (equations preserved, no PDF parsing artifacts), then produce a structured reading digest — core contribution, method walkthrough, key equations, and notable appendix/footnote details. Trigger when the user pastes an arxiv link or ID and asks to read, summarize, explain, or understand a paper. Do NOT trigger for implementation requests ("implement this paper", "reproduce") — that is the paper-to-code skill. Inspired by Karpathy's read-arxiv-paper approach. - **read-arxiv-paper**: Fetch an arXiv paper's TeX source into raw/ and compile it into the wiki. Use when the user gives an arXiv URL or ID to read or ingest. ([hiennguyen9874/llm-wiki](https://github.com/hiennguyen9874/llm-wiki/tree/HEAD/.pi/skills/read-arxiv-paper) · ★1)
- **read-arxiv-paper**: Read arXiv or AlphaXiv papers for summary, critique, comparison, and implementation relevance. Use when the user shares a paper link or ID and wants the method, evidence, caveats, or practical takeaways explained clearly. ([jojo23333/workbench](https://github.com/jojo23333/workbench/tree/HEAD/skills/read-arxiv-paper) · ★1)
- **read-arxiv-paper**: Use this skill when asked to read an arxiv paper given an arxiv URL. Fetches the LaTeX source (not the PDF), unpacks it, locates the entrypoint, reads the full paper recursively through \input{}/\include{} directives, produces a project-contextualized summary at ./knowledge/summary_{tag}.md, and syncs it to Obsidian via the Local REST API plugin.
